import sys
import json
import logging
import traceback

# Setup logging ke file agar stderr tidak mengganggu IPC stdio
logging.basicConfig(
    filename="backend_log.txt",
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s"
)

# In-memory storage untuk data referensi
master_kegiatan_db = []
master_rekening_db = []

def handle_command(action, payload):
    """
    Penanganan perintah/aksi yang dikirim dari Electron Main Process
    """
    global master_kegiatan_db, master_rekening_db
    action = action.replace("-", "_")  # Dukung format kebab-case dan snake_case
    
    try:
        if action == "ping":
            return {"status": "success", "message": "pong"}

        elif action == "get_pengaturan":
            # Return dummy / data pengaturan awal jika belum ada DB
            return {
                "status": "success",
                "data": {
                    "nama_sekolah": "SD Negeri 1 Contoh",
                    "nama_kepala_sekolah": "Kepala Sekolah, S.Pd.",
                    "nip_kepala_sekolah": "19700101 199001 1 001",
                    "nama_bendahara": "Bendahara, A.Md.",
                    "nip_bendahara": "19850101 201001 2 002",
                    "nama_pengurus_barang": "-",
                    "nip_pengurus_barang": "-"
                }
            }

        elif action == "get_pemda":
            return {
                "status": "success",
                "data": {
                    "nama_pemda": "PEMERINTAH KABUPATEN / KOTA",
                    "nama_sekolah": "SD NEGERI 1 CONTOH",
                    "alamat_sekolah": "Jl. Pendidikan No. 123",
                    "tempat_surat": "Kota Contoh",
                    "logo_pemda": ""
                }
            }

        elif action == "get_transaksi":
            return {"status": "success", "data": []}

        elif action == "get_master_kegiatan":
            return {"status": "success", "data": master_kegiatan_db}

        elif action == "get_master_rekening_belanja":
            return {"status": "success", "data": master_rekening_db}

        elif action == "save_master_kegiatan_bulk":
            data = payload.get("data", [])
            master_kegiatan_db = data
            return {
                "status": "success",
                "saved_count": len(data),
                "data": master_kegiatan_db,
                "message": f"Berhasil menyimpan {len(data)} data kegiatan"
            }

        elif action == "save_master_rekening_belanja_bulk":
            data = payload.get("data", [])
            for idx, item in enumerate(data, start=1):
                if "id" not in item or not item["id"]:
                    item["id"] = idx
            master_rekening_db = data
            return {
                "status": "success",
                "saved_count": len(data),
                "data": master_rekening_db,
                "message": f"Berhasil menyimpan {len(data)} data rekening belanja"
            }

        elif action == "delete_master_kegiatan":
            kode = payload.get("kode_kegiatan")
            master_kegiatan_db = [k for k in master_kegiatan_db if k.get("kode_kegiatan") != kode]
            return {"status": "success", "message": "Master kegiatan berhasil dihapus"}

        elif action == "delete_master_rekening":
            req_id = payload.get("id")
            master_rekening_db = [r for r in master_rekening_db if r.get("id") != req_id]
            return {"status": "success", "message": "Master rekening berhasil dihapus"}

        elif action == "get_kwitansi":
            return {"status": "success", "data": []}

        elif action == "get_kwitansi_detail":
            return {"status": "success", "data": {}}

        elif action == "get_rekap_transaksi":
            return {"status": "success", "data": []}

        elif action in ["save_pengaturan", "save_pemda", "save_transaksi"]:
            return {"status": "success", "message": "Data berhasil disimpan"}

        else:
            return {"status": "error", "message": f"Aksi '{action}' tidak dikenal."}

    except Exception as e:
        logging.error(f"Error handling action '{action}': {traceback.format_exc()}")
        return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}

    if hasattr(sys.stdin, "reconfigure"):
        sys.stdin.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
    if hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
        sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")

    while True:
        try:
            line = sys.stdin.readline()
            if not line:

            line = line.strip()
            if not line:

Hal ini terjadi karena data referensi saat ini hanya disimpan dalam memori sementara (RAM) di backend Python (`master_kegiatan_db = []` dan `master_rekening_db = []`). Ketika aplikasi ditutup, proses backend Python dihentikan sehingga data dalam memori tersebut hilang.

Untuk mengatasinya, kita perlu menyimpan (*persist*) data tersebut ke dalam file lokal (misalnya `data_store.json`) secara otomatis setiap kali ada penambahan/penghapusan data, serta memuatnya kembali saat aplikasi pertama kali dibuka.

Berikut adalah perbaikan file `backend/main.py`:

)

master_rekening_db = []

    if os.path.exists(DATA_FILE):
        try:
            with open(DATA_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                data = json.load(f)
                master_kegiatan_db = data.get("master_kegiatan", [])
                master_rekening_db = data.get("master_rekening", [])
                logging.info(f"Data berhasil dimuat dari {DATA_FILE}")
        except Exception as e:

            "master_kegiatan": master_kegiatan_db,
            "master_rekening": master_rekening_db
        }
        with open(DATA_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
            logging.info(f"Data berhasil disimpan ke {DATA_FILE}")
    except Exception as e:
        logging.error(f"Gagal menyimpan data ke {DATA_FILE}: {e}")

```python
import sys
import json
import logging
import traceback
import os

# Setup logging ke file agar stderr tidak mengganggu IPC stdio
logging.basicConfig(
    filename="backend_log.txt",
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s"
)

DATA_FILE = "data_store.json"

# In-memory storage untuk data referensi dan transaksi
master_kegiatan_db = []
master_rekening_db = []
transaksi_db = []
kwitansi_db = []
pengaturan_db = {
    "nama_sekolah": "SD Negeri 1 Contoh",
    "nama_kepala_sekolah": "Kepala Sekolah, S.Pd.",
    "nip_kepala_sekolah": "19700101 199001 1 001",
    "nama_bendahara": "Bendahara, A.Md.",
    "nip_bendahara": "19850101 201001 2 002",
    "nama_pengurus_barang": "-",
    "nip_pengurus_barang": "-",
    "alamat_sekolah": "Jl. Pendidikan No. 123"
}
pemda_db = {
    "nama_pemda": "PEMERINTAH KABUPATEN / KOTA",
    "nama_sekolah": "SD NEGERI 1 CONTOH",
    "alamat_sekolah": "Jl. Pendidikan No. 123",
    "tempat_surat": "Kota Contoh",
    "logo_pemda": "",
    "logo_sekolah": ""
}

def load_data():
    """Memuat data dari file JSON lokal jika ada."""
    global master_kegiatan_db, master_rekening_db, transaksi_db, kwitansi_db, pengaturan_db, pemda_db
    if os.path.exists(DATA_FILE):
        try:
            with open(DATA_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                data = json.load(f)
                master_kegiatan_db = data.get("master_kegiatan", [])
                master_rekening_db = data.get("master_rekening", [])
                transaksi_db = data.get("transaksi", [])
                kwitansi_db = data.get("kwitansi", [])
                if "pengaturan" in data:
                    pengaturan_db = data["pengaturan"]
                if "pemda" in data:
                    pemda_db = data["pemda"]
                logging.info(f"Data berhasil dimuat dari {DATA_FILE}")
        except Exception as e:
            logging.error(f"Gagal memuat data dari {DATA_FILE}: {e}")

def save_data():
    """Menyimpan data saat ini ke file JSON lokal."""
    try:
        data = {
            "master_kegiatan": master_kegiatan_db,
            "master_rekening": master_rekening_db,
            "transaksi": transaksi_db,
            "kwitansi": kwitansi_db,
            "pengaturan": pengaturan_db,
            "pemda": pemda_db
        }
        with open(DATA_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
            logging.info(f"Data berhasil disimpan ke {DATA_FILE}")
    except Exception as e:
        logging.error(f"Gagal menyimpan data ke {DATA_FILE}: {e}")

def handle_command(action, payload):
    """Penanganan perintah/aksi yang dikirim dari Electron Main Process."""
    global master_kegiatan_db, master_rekening_db, transaksi_db, kwitansi_db, pengaturan_db, pemda_db
    action = action.replace("-", "_")

    try:
        if action == "ping":
            return {"status": "success", "message": "pong"}

        elif action == "get_pengaturan":
            return {"status": "success", "data": pengaturan_db}

        elif action == "save_pengaturan":
            if isinstance(payload, dict):
                pengaturan_db.update(payload)
                save_data()
            return {"status": "success", "message": "Pengaturan berhasil disimpan"}

        elif action == "get_pemda":
            return {"status": "success", "data": pemda_db}

        elif action == "save_pemda":
            if isinstance(payload, dict):
                pemda_db.update(payload)
                save_data()
            return {"status": "success", "message": "Data Pemda/Kop berhasil disimpan"}

        elif action == "get_transaksi":
            return {"status": "success", "data": transaksi_db}

        elif action == "save_transaksi":
            force = payload.get("force", False)
            if not force:
                return {
                    "status": "preview",
                    "would_delete_rows": len(transaksi_db),
                    "would_delete_bpu_rows": 0,
                    "would_insert": len(payload.get("transaksi", []))
                }
            transaksi_db = payload.get("transaksi", [])
            save_data()
            return {"status": "success", "saved_count": len(transaksi_db), "message": "Transaksi berhasil disimpan"}

        elif action == "clear_all_transaksi":
            transaksi_db = []
            kwitansi_db = []
            save_data()
            return {"status": "success", "message": "Semua data transaksi BKU berhasil dikosongkan"}

        elif action == "delete_transaksi":
            no_bukti = payload.get("no_bukti")
            transaksi_db = [t for t in transaksi_db if t.get("no_bukti") != no_bukti]
            save_data()
            return {"status": "success", "message": f"Transaksi {no_bukti} berhasil dihapus"}

        elif action == "get_master_kegiatan":
            return {"status": "success", "data": master_kegiatan_db}

        elif action == "save_master_kegiatan_bulk":
            data = payload.get("data", [])
            master_kegiatan_db = data
            save_data()
            return {
                "status": "success",
                "saved_count": len(data),
                "data": master_kegiatan_db,
                "message": f"Berhasil menyimpan {len(data)} data kegiatan"
            }

        elif action == "delete_master_kegiatan":
            kode = payload.get("kode_kegiatan")
            master_kegiatan_db = [k for k in master_kegiatan_db if k.get("kode_kegiatan") != kode]
            save_data()
            return {"status": "success", "message": "Master kegiatan berhasil dihapus"}

        elif action == "get_master_rekening_belanja":
            return {"status": "success", "data": master_rekening_db}

        elif action == "save_master_rekening_belanja_bulk":
            data = payload.get("data", [])
            for idx, item in enumerate(data, start=1):
                if "id" not in item or not item["id"]:
                    item["id"] = idx
            master_rekening_db = data
            save_data()
            return {
                "status": "success",
                "saved_count": len(data),
                "data": master_rekening_db,
                "message": f"Berhasil menyimpan {len(data)} data rekening belanja"
            }

        elif action == "delete_master_rekening":
            req_id = payload.get("id")
            master_rekening_db = [r for r in master_rekening_db if r.get("id") != req_id]
            save_data()
            return {"status": "success", "message": "Master rekening berhasil dihapus"}

        elif action == "get_kwitansi":
            return {"status": "success", "data": kwitansi_db}

        elif action == "get_kwitansi_detail":
            kw_id = payload.get("kwitansi_id")
            found = next((k for k in kwitansi_db if k.get("id") == kw_id), {})
            return {"status": "success", "data": found}

        elif action == "update_kwitansi_detail":
            kw_id = payload.get("kwitansi_id")
            for k in kwitansi_db:
                if k.get("id") == kw_id:
                    k.update(payload)
                    break
            save_data()
            return {"status": "success", "message": "Detail kwitansi berhasil diperbarui"}

        elif action == "upload_foto_kwitansi":
            kw_id = payload.get("kwitansi_id")
            files = payload.get("files", [])
            jenis_foto = payload.get("jenis_foto", "barang")
            for k in kwitansi_db:
                if k.get("id") == kw_id:
                    if "foto_bukti" not in k or not isinstance(k["foto_bukti"], list):
                        k["foto_bukti"] = []
                    for f in files:
                        path_url = f"data:{f.get('mime_type','image/png')};base64,{f.get('content','')}"
                        k["foto_bukti"].append({"path": path_url, "jenis": jenis_foto})
                    break
            save_data()
            return {"status": "success", "message": f"Berhasil mengunggah {len(files)} foto"}

        elif action == "delete_foto_kwitansi":
            kw_id = payload.get("kwitansi_id")
            idx = payload.get("foto_index", 0)
            for k in kwitansi_db:
                if k.get("id") == kw_id and "foto_bukti" in k and isinstance(k["foto_bukti"], list):
                    if 0 <= idx < len(k["foto_bukti"]):
                        k["foto_bukti"].pop(idx)
                    break
            save_data()
            return {"status": "success", "message": "Foto berhasil dihapus"}

        elif action == "create_manual_kwitansi":
            no_bukti = payload.get("no_bukti", f"N{len(kwitansi_db)+1}")
            new_id = len(kwitansi_db) + 1
            kw_item = {
                "id": new_id,
                "nomor_kwitansi": no_bukti,
                "no_bukti": no_bukti,
                "nama_kegiatan": payload.get("nama_kegiatan", "Kegiatan Manual"),
                "tanggal": payload.get("tanggal", ""),
                "jumlah": sum(i.get("pengeluaran", 0) for i in payload.get("uraian", [])),
                "uraian": payload.get("uraian", []),
                "no_bast": payload.get("no_bast", ""),
                "nama_toko": payload.get("nama_toko", ""),
                "npwp_toko": payload.get("npwp_toko", ""),
                "alamat_toko": payload.get("alamat_toko", ""),
                "foto_bukti": []
            }
            kwitansi_db.append(kw_item)
            save_data()
            return {"status": "success", "message": "Data BKU manual berhasil ditambahkan"}

        elif action == "get_rekap_transaksi":
            groups = {}
            for t in transaksi_db:
                r_code = t.get("kode_rekening", "LAINNYA")
                if r_code not in groups:
                    groups[r_code] = {"rekap": r_code, "total_pengeluaran": 0, "items": []}
                peng = float(t.get("pengeluaran", 0) or 0)
                groups[r_code]["total_pengeluaran"] += peng
                groups[r_code]["items"].append(t)
            return {"status": "success", "data": list(groups.values())}

        else:
            return {"status": "error", "message": f"Aksi '{action}' tidak dikenal."}

    except Exception as e:
        logging.error(f"Error handling action '{action}': {traceback.format_exc()}")
        return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}

def main():
    logging.info("Python Backend Started (IPC JSON stdio mode)")
    load_data()
    
    if hasattr(sys.stdin, "reconfigure"):
        sys.stdin.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
    if hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
        sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")

    while True:
        try:
            line = sys.stdin.readline()
            if not line:
                break
            line = line.strip()
            if not line:
                continue

            request = json.loads(line)
            req_id = request.get("id")
            action = request.get("action")
            payload = request.get("payload", {})

            result = handle_command(action, payload)
            response = {"id": req_id, "result": result}

            sys.stdout.write(json.dumps(response, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
            sys.stdout.flush()

        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
            logging.error(f"JSON Parse Error: {e}")
        except Exception as e:
            logging.error(f"Unhandled Exception: {traceback.format_exc()}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

import * as XLSX from "xlsx";

let appVersion = "2.0.0";
document.title = `Aplikasi SPJ BOSP v${appVersion}`;

// State
let bkuData = null;
let pengaturan = {
    nama_sekolah: "",
    nama_kepala_sekolah: "",
    nip_kepala_sekolah: "",
    nama_bendahara: "",
    nip_bendahara: "",
    nama_pengurus_barang: "",
    nip_pengurus_barang: "",
    alamat_sekolah: ""
};
let masterKegiatan = [];
let masterRekeningBelanja = [];
let activeTab = "pengaturan";

// Helper Conversion to Base64
function fileToBase64(file) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        reader.onload = () => {
            const result = reader.result;
            const base64 = result.split(',')[1];
            resolve({
                filename: file.name,
                content: base64,
                mime_type: file.type
            });
        };
        reader.onerror = error => reject(error);
    });
}

// Helper Download Blob from Base64
function downloadBase64File(base64Data, fileName, mimeType = "application/pdf") {
    const binary = atob(base64Data);
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i++) {
        bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
    }
    const blob = new Blob([bytes], { type: mimeType });
    const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = url;
    a.download = fileName;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
    window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

// Helper Format Rupiah
function formatRupiah(num) {
    return new Intl.NumberFormat("id-ID", { style: "currency", currency: "IDR", minimumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(num);
}

backend\main.py
```python
import sys
import json
import logging
import traceback
import os

# Setup logging ke file agar stderr tidak mengganggu IPC stdio
logging.basicConfig(
    filename="backend_log.txt",
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s"
)

DATA_FILE = "data_store.json"

# In-memory storage untuk data referensi
master_kegiatan_db = []
master_rekening_db = []

def load_data():
    """Memuat data dari file JSON jika ada."""
    global master_kegiatan_db, master_rekening_db
    if os.path.exists(DATA_FILE):
        try:
            with open(DATA_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                data = json.load(f)
                master_kegiatan_db = data.get("master_kegiatan", [])
                master_rekening_db = data.get("master_rekening", [])
                logging.info(f"Data berhasil dimuat dari {DATA_FILE}")
        except Exception as e:
            logging.error(f"Gagal memuat data dari {DATA_FILE}: {e}")

def save_data():
    """Menyimpan data saat ini ke file JSON."""
    try:
        data = {
            "master_kegiatan": master_kegiatan_db,
            "master_rekening": master_rekening_db
        }
        with open(DATA_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
            logging.info(f"Data berhasil disimpan ke {DATA_FILE}")
    except Exception as e:
        logging.error(f"Gagal menyimpan data ke {DATA_FILE}: {e}")

def handle_command(action, payload):
    """
    Penanganan perintah/aksi yang dikirim dari Electron Main Process
    """
    global master_kegiatan_db, master_rekening_db
    action = action.replace("-", "_")  # Dukung format kebab-case dan snake_case
    
    try:
        if action == "ping":
            return {"status": "success", "message": "pong"}

        elif action == "get_pengaturan":
            # Return dummy / data pengaturan awal jika belum ada DB
            return {
                "status": "success",
                "data": {
                    "nama_sekolah": "SD Negeri 1 Contoh",
                    "nama_kepala_sekolah": "Kepala Sekolah, S.Pd.",
                    "nip_kepala_sekolah": "19700101 199001 1 001",
                    "nama_bendahara": "Bendahara, A.Md.",
                    "nip_bendahara": "19850101 201001 2 002",
                    "nama_pengurus_barang": "-",
                    "nip_pengurus_barang": "-"
                }
            }

        elif action == "get_pemda":
            return {
                "status": "success",
                "data": {
                    "nama_pemda": "PEMERINTAH KABUPATEN / KOTA",
                    "nama_sekolah": "SD NEGERI 1 CONTOH",
                    "alamat_sekolah": "Jl. Pendidikan No. 123",
                    "tempat_surat": "Kota Contoh",
                    "logo_pemda": ""
                }
            }

        elif action == "get_transaksi":
            return {"status": "success", "data": []}

        elif action == "get_master_kegiatan":
            return {"status": "success", "data": master_kegiatan_db}

        elif action == "get_master_rekening_belanja":
            return {"status": "success", "data": master_rekening_db}

        elif action == "save_master_kegiatan_bulk":
            data = payload.get("data", [])
            master_kegiatan_db = data
            save_data()
            return {
                "status": "success",
                "saved_count": len(data),
                "data": master_kegiatan_db,
                "message": f"Berhasil menyimpan {len(data)} data kegiatan"
            }

        elif action == "save_master_rekening_belanja_bulk":
            data = payload.get("data", [])
            for idx, item in enumerate(data, start=1):
                if "id" not in item or not item["id"]:
                    item["id"] = idx
            master_rekening_db = data
            save_data()
            return {
                "status": "success",
                "saved_count": len(data),
                "data": master_rekening_db,
                "message": f"Berhasil menyimpan {len(data)} data rekening belanja"
            }

        elif action == "delete_master_kegiatan":
            kode = payload.get("kode_kegiatan")
            master_kegiatan_db = [k for k in master_kegiatan_db if k.get("kode_kegiatan") != kode]
            save_data()
            return {"status": "success", "message": "Master kegiatan berhasil dihapus"}

        elif action == "delete_master_rekening":
            req_id = payload.get("id")
            master_rekening_db = [r for r in master_rekening_db if r.get("id") != req_id]
            save_data()
            return {"status": "success", "message": "Master rekening berhasil dihapus"}

        elif action == "get_kwitansi":
            return {"status": "success", "data": []}

        elif action == "get_kwitansi_detail":
            return {"status": "success", "data": {}}

        elif action == "get_rekap_transaksi":
            return {"status": "success", "data": []}

        elif action in ["save_pengaturan", "save_pemda", "save_transaksi"]:
            return {"status": "success", "message": "Data berhasil disimpan"}

        else:
            return {"status": "error", "message": f"Aksi '{action}' tidak dikenal."}

    except Exception as e:
        logging.error(f"Error handling action '{action}': {traceback.format_exc()}")
        return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}


def main():
    logging.info("Python Backend Started (IPC JSON stdio mode)")
    
    # Memuat data tersimpan saat startup
    load_data()
    
    # Memastikan buffer stdin/stdout menggunakan UTF-8
    if hasattr(sys.stdin, "reconfigure"):
        sys.stdin.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")
    if hasattr(sys.stdout, "reconfigure"):
        sys.stdout.reconfigure(encoding="utf-8")

    while True:
        try:
            line = sys.stdin.readline()
            if not line:
                break
            
            line = line.strip()
            if not line:
                continue

            request = json.loads(line)
            req_id = request.get("id")
            action = request.get("action")
            payload = request.get("payload", {})

            result = handle_command(action, payload)

            response = {
                "id": req_id,
                "result": result
            }

            sys.stdout.write(json.dumps(response, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
            sys.stdout.flush()

        except json.JSONDecodeError as e:
            logging.error(f"JSON Parse Error: {e}")
        except Exception as e:
            logging.error(f"Unhandled Exception: {traceback.format_exc()}")


if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()
```

2. **Perbaikan di `frontend/app.js`**:

import sys
import json
import logging
import traceback
import os

# Setup logging ke file agar stderr tidak mengganggu IPC stdio
logging.basicConfig(
    filename="backend_log.txt",
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s"
)

DATA_FILE = "data_store.json"

# In-memory storage untuk data referensi dan pengaturan
master_kegiatan_db = []
master_rekening_db = []
pengaturan_db = {
    "nama_sekolah": "SD Negeri 1 Contoh",
    "nama_kepala_sekolah": "Kepala Sekolah, S.Pd.",
    "nip_kepala_sekolah": "19700101 199001 1 001",
    "nama_bendahara": "Bendahara, A.Md.",
    "nip_bendahara": "19850101 201001 2 002",
    "nama_pengurus_barang": "-",
    "nip_pengurus_barang": "-",
    "alamat_sekolah": "Jl. Pendidikan No. 123"
}
pemda_db = {
    "nama_pemda": "PEMERINTAH KABUPATEN / KOTA",
    "nama_sekolah": "SD Negeri 1 Contoh",
    "alamat_sekolah": "Jl. Pendidikan No. 123",
    "tempat_surat": "Kota Contoh",
    "logo_pemda": "",
    "logo_sekolah": ""
}

def load_data():
    """Memuat data dari file JSON jika ada."""
    global master_kegiatan_db, master_rekening_db, pengaturan_db, pemda_db
    if os.path.exists(DATA_FILE):
        try:
            with open(DATA_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                data = json.load(f)
                master_kegiatan_db = data.get("master_kegiatan", [])
                master_rekening_db = data.get("master_rekening", [])
                if "pengaturan" in data:
                    pengaturan_db.update(data.get("pengaturan", {}))
                if "pemda" in data:
                    pemda_db.update(data.get("pemda", {}))
                logging.info(f"Data berhasil dimuat dari {DATA_FILE}")
        except Exception as e:
            logging.error(f"Gagal memuat data dari {DATA_FILE}: {e}")

def save_data():
    """Menyimpan data saat ini ke file JSON."""
    try:
        data = {
            "master_kegiatan": master_kegiatan_db,
            "master_rekening": master_rekening_db,
            "pengaturan": pengaturan_db,
            "pemda": pemda_db
        }
        with open(DATA_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
            logging.info(f"Data berhasil disimpan ke {DATA_FILE}")
    except Exception as e:
        logging.error(f"Gagal menyimpan data ke {DATA_FILE}: {e}")

def handle_command(action, payload):
    """
    Penanganan perintah/aksi yang dikirim dari Electron Main Process
    """
    global master_kegiatan_db, master_rekening_db, pengaturan_db, pemda_db
    action = action.replace("-", "_")  # Dukung format kebab-case dan snake_case

        elif action == "get_pengaturan":

import sys
import json
import logging
import traceback
import os

# Setup logging ke file agar stderr tidak mengganggu IPC stdio
logging.basicConfig(
    filename="backend_log.txt",
    level=logging.INFO,
    format="%(asctime)s [%(levelname)s] %(message)s"
)

DATA_FILE = "data_store.json"

# In-memory storage untuk data referensi dan pengaturan
master_kegiatan_db = []
master_rekening_db = []
pengaturan_db = {
    "nama_sekolah": "SD Negeri 1 Contoh",
    "nama_kepala_sekolah": "Kepala Sekolah, S.Pd.",
    "nip_kepala_sekolah": "19700101 199001 1 001",
    "nama_bendahara": "Bendahara, A.Md.",
    "nip_bendahara": "19850101 201001 2 002",
    "nama_pengurus_barang": "-",
    "nip_pengurus_barang": "-",
    "alamat_sekolah": "Jl. Pendidikan No. 123"
}
pemda_db = {
    "nama_pemda": "PEMERINTAH KABUPATEN / KOTA",
    "nama_sekolah": "SD Negeri 1 Contoh",
    "alamat_sekolah": "Jl. Pendidikan No. 123",
    "tempat_surat": "Kota Contoh",
    "logo_pemda": "",
    "logo_sekolah": ""
}

def load_data():
    """Memuat data dari file JSON jika ada."""
    global master_kegiatan_db, master_rekening_db, pengaturan_db, pemda_db
    if os.path.exists(DATA_FILE):
        try:
            with open(DATA_FILE, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                data = json.load(f)
                master_kegiatan_db = data.get("master_kegiatan", [])
                master_rekening_db = data.get("master_rekening", [])
                if "pengaturan" in data:
                    pengaturan_db.update(data.get("pengaturan", {}))
                if "pemda" in data:
                    pemda_db.update(data.get("pemda", {}))
                logging.info(f"Data berhasil dimuat dari {DATA_FILE}")
        except Exception as e:
            logging.error(f"Gagal memuat data dari {DATA_FILE}: {e}")

def save_data():
    """Menyimpan data saat ini ke file JSON."""
    try:
        data = {
            "master_kegiatan": master_kegiatan_db,
            "master_rekening": master_rekening_db,
            "pengaturan": pengaturan_db,
            "pemda": pemda_db
        }
        with open(DATA_FILE, "w", encoding="utf-8") as f:
            json.dump(data, f, ensure_ascii=False, indent=2)
            logging.info(f"Data berhasil disimpan ke {DATA_FILE}")
    except Exception as e:
        logging.error(f"Gagal menyimpan data ke {DATA_FILE}: {e}")

def handle_command(action, payload):
    """
    Penanganan perintah/aksi yang dikirim dari Electron Main Process
    """
    global master_kegiatan_db, master_rekening_db, pengaturan_db, pemda_db
    action = action.replace("-", "_")  # Dukung format kebab-case dan snake_case
    
    try:
        if action == "ping":
            return {"status": "success", "message": "pong"}

        elif action == "get_pengaturan":
            return {
                "status": "success",
                "data": pengaturan_db
            }

        elif action == "save_pengaturan":
            if isinstance(payload, dict):
                pengaturan_db.update(payload)
                # Sinkronkan ke data pemda
                if "nama_sekolah" in payload:
                    pemda_db["nama_sekolah"] = payload["nama_sekolah"]
                if "alamat_sekolah" in payload:
                    pemda_db["alamat_sekolah"] = payload["alamat_sekolah"]
                save_data()
            return {"status": "success", "message": "Pengaturan berhasil disimpan"}

        elif action == "get_pemda":
            return {
                "status": "success",
                "data": pemda_db
            }

        elif action == "save_pemda":
            if isinstance(payload, dict):
                pemda_db.update(payload)
                # Sinkronkan ke data pengaturan
                if "nama_sekolah" in payload:
                    pengaturan_db["nama_sekolah"] = payload["nama_sekolah"]
                if "alamat_sekolah" in payload:
                    pengaturan_db["alamat_sekolah"] = payload["alamat_sekolah"]
                save_data()
            return {"status": "success", "message": "Data Pemda & Logo berhasil disimpan"}

        elif action == "get_transaksi":
            return {"status": "success", "data": []}

        elif action == "get_master_kegiatan":
            return {"status": "success", "data": master_kegiatan_db}

        elif action == "get_master_rekening_belanja":
            return {"status": "success", "data": master_rekening_db}

        elif action == "save_master_kegiatan_bulk":
            data = payload.get("data", [])
            master_kegiatan_db = data
            save_data()
            return {
                "status": "success",
                "saved_count": len(data),
                "data": master_kegiatan_db,
                "message": f"Berhasil menyimpan {len(data)} data kegiatan"
            }

        elif action == "save_master_rekening_belanja_bulk":
            data = payload.get("data", [])
            for idx, item in enumerate(data, start=1):
                if "id" not in item or not item["id"]:
                    item["id"] = idx
            master_rekening_db = data
            save_data()
            return {
                "status": "success",
                "saved_count": len(data),
                "data": master_rekening_db,
                "message": f"Berhasil menyimpan {len(data)} data rekening belanja"
            }

        elif action == "delete_master_kegiatan":
            kode = payload.get("kode_kegiatan")
            master_kegiatan_db = [k for k in master_kegiatan_db if k.get("kode_kegiatan") != kode]
            save_data()
            return {"status": "success", "message": "Master kegiatan berhasil dihapus"}

        elif action == "delete_master_rekening":
            req_id = payload.get("id")
            master_rekening_db = [r for r in master_rekening_db if r.get("id") != req_id]
            save_data()
            return {"status": "success", "message": "Master rekening berhasil dihapus"}

        elif action == "get_kwitansi":
            return {"status": "success", "data": []}

        elif action == "get_kwitansi_detail":
            return {"status": "success", "data": {}}

        elif action == "get_rekap_transaksi":
            return {"status": "success", "data": []}

        elif action == "save_transaksi":
            return {"status": "success", "message": "Data berhasil disimpan"}

        else:
            return {"status": "error", "message": f"Aksi '{action}' tidak dikenal"}

    except Exception as e:
        logging.error(f"Error handling command {action}: {e}\n{traceback.format_exc()}")
        return {"status": "error", "message": str(e)}

def main():
    load_data()
    logging.info("Backend stdio service started")

    while True:
        try:
            line = sys.stdin.readline()
            if not line:
                break
            line = line.strip()
            if not line:
                continue

            req = json.loads(line)
            req_id = req.get("id")
            action = req.get("action")
            payload = req.get("payload", {})

            result = handle_command(action, payload)

            response = {
                "id": req_id,
                "result": result
            }

            sys.stdout.write(json.dumps(response, ensure_ascii=False) + "\n")
            sys.stdout.flush()

        except Exception as e:
            logging.error(f"Error pada loop utama: {e}\n{traceback.format_exc()}")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    main()

```javascript
import * as XLSX from "xlsx";

let appVersion = "-";
document.title = `Aplikasi SPJ BOSP v${appVersion}`;

// State
let bkuData = null;
let pengaturan = {
    nama_sekolah: "",
    nama_kepala_sekolah: "",
    nip_kepala_sekolah: "",
    nama_bendahara: "",
    nip_bendahara: "",
    nama_pengurus_barang: "",
    nip_pengurus_barang: "",
    alamat_sekolah: ""
};
let masterKegiatan = [];
let masterRekeningBelanja = [];
let activeTab = "pengaturan";

// Helper Conversion to Base64
function fileToBase64(file) {
    return new Promise((resolve, reject) => {
        const reader = new FileReader();
        reader.readAsDataURL(file);
        reader.onload = () => {
            const result = reader.result;
            const base64 = result.split(',')[1];
            resolve({
                filename: file.name,
                content: base64,
                mime_type: file.type
            });
        };
        reader.onerror = error => reject(error);
    });
}

// Helper Download Blob from Base64
function downloadBase64File(base64Data, fileName, mimeType = "application/pdf") {
    const binary = atob(base64Data);
    const bytes = new Uint8Array(binary.length);
    for (let i = 0; i < binary.length; i++) {
        bytes[i] = binary.charCodeAt(i);
    }
    const blob = new Blob([bytes], { type: mimeType });
    const url = window.URL.createObjectURL(blob);
    const a = document.createElement("a");
    a.href = url;
    a.download = fileName;
    document.body.appendChild(a);
    a.click();
    document.body.removeChild(a);
    window.URL.revokeObjectURL(url);
}

// Helper Format Rupiah
function formatRupiah(num) {
    return new Intl.NumberFormat("id-ID", { style: "currency", currency: "IDR", minimumFractionDigits: 0 }).format(num);
}

// --- CUSTOM ASYNC CONFIRM MODAL ---
function showCustomConfirm(title, message) {
    return new Promise((resolve) => {
        const modalId = `custom-confirm-${Date.now()}`;
        
        const warningIconSvg = `
            <svg class="icon icon-lg" style="stroke: var(--danger); margin-bottom: 12px;" viewBox="0 0 24 24">
                <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle>
                <line x1="12" y1="8" x2="12" y2="12"></line>
                <line x1="12" y1="16" x2="12.01" y2="16"></line>
            </svg>
        `;

        const modalHtml = `
            <div id="${modalId}" style="
                position: fixed; top: 0; left: 0; width: 100vw; height: 100vh;
                background: rgba(15, 23, 42, 0.3); backdrop-filter: blur(4px);
                display: flex; align-items: center; justify-content: center;
                z-index: 99999; animation: confirmFadeIn 0.2s ease forwards;
            ">
                <div class="card d-flex flex-col align-center text-center" style="
                    width: 90%; max-width: 400px; margin-bottom: 0;
                    box-shadow: 0 10px 25px -5px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.1);
                    animation: confirmSlideUp 0.25s cubic-bezier(0.16, 1, 0.3, 1) forwards;
                ">
                    ${warningIconSvg}
                    <h3 class="mt-0 mb-2" style="font-weight: 700; font-size: 18px;">${title}</h3>
                    <p style="color: var(--text-muted); font-size: 13px; margin-bottom: 24px; line-height: 1.5;">${message}</p>
                    
                    <div class="d-flex gap-2 w-100">
                        <button id="${modalId}-cancel" class="btn btn-secondary flex-1">Batal</button>
                        <button id="${modalId}-confirm" class="btn btn-danger flex-1">Ya, Hapus</button>
                    </div>
                </div>
            </div>
            <style>
                @keyframes confirmFadeIn { from { opacity: 0; } to { opacity: 1; } }
                @keyframes confirmSlideUp { from { transform: translateY(16px); opacity: 0; } to { transform: translateY(0); opacity: 1; } }
                @keyframes confirmFadeOut { to { opacity: 0; } }
            </style>
        `;

        const wrapper = document.createElement('div');
        wrapper.innerHTML = modalHtml;
        document.body.appendChild(wrapper);

        const modalElement = document.getElementById(modalId);

        const closeModal = (result) => {
            modalElement.style.animation = "confirmFadeOut 0.15s ease forwards";
            setTimeout(() => {
                wrapper.remove();
                resolve(result);
            }, 120);
        };

        document.getElementById(`${modalId}-confirm`).addEventListener('click', () => closeModal(true));
        document.getElementById(`${modalId}-cancel`).addEventListener('click', () => closeModal(false));
        
        modalElement.addEventListener('click', (e) => {
            if (e.target === modalElement) closeModal(false);
        });
    });
}

// --- SHOW INLINE MESSAGE ALERT ---
function showMessage(type, text) {
    const msgDiv = document.getElementById("message");
    if (!msgDiv) return;

    let alertClass = "alert-info";
    let titleText = "Informasi";
    let svgIcon = `<svg class="icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><line x1="12" y1="16" x2="12" y2="12"></line><line x1="12" y1="8" x2="12.01" y2="8"></line></svg>`;
    let customStyle = "";

    if (type === 'warning') {
        alertClass = "alert-warning";
        titleText = "Perhatian";
        svgIcon = `<svg class="icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10.29 3.86L1.82 18a2 2 0 0 0 1.71 3h16.94a2 2 0 0 0 1.71-3L13.71 3.86a2 2 0 0 0-3.42 0z"></path><line x1="12" y1="9" x2="12" y2="13"></line><line x1="12" y1="17" x2="12.01" y2="17"></line></svg>`;
    } else if (type === 'success') {
        titleText = "Berhasil";
        svgIcon = `<svg class="icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M22 11.08V12a10 10 0 1 1-5.93-9.14"></path><polyline points="22 4 12 14.01 9 11.01"></polyline></svg>`;
        customStyle = `background: #ecfdf5; border: 1px solid #a7f3d0; color: #065f46;`;
    } else if (type === 'danger' || type === 'error') {
        titleText = "Kesalahan";
        svgIcon = `<svg class="icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><line x1="15" y1="9" x2="9" y2="15"></line><line x1="9" y1="9" x2="15" y2="15"></line></svg>`;
        customStyle = `background: #fef2f2; border: 1px solid #fecaca; color: #991b1b;`;
    }

    msgDiv.innerHTML = `
        <div class="${alertClass} mb-3" style="${customStyle} animation: fadeIn 0.3s ease;">
            <div class="alert-title" style="${type === 'success' || type === 'danger' || type === 'error' ? 'color: inherit;' : ''}">
                ${svgIcon} ${titleText}
            </div>
            <p class="alert-text" style="${type === 'success' || type === 'danger' || type === 'error' ? 'color: inherit;' : ''}">${text}</p>
        </div>
    `;

    setTimeout(() => msgDiv.innerHTML = "", 3000);
}

// --- SHOW NOTIFICATION TOAST ---
function showToast(title, message, type = 'info', actionText = null, actionCallback = null) {
    let container = document.getElementById('toast-container');
    if (!container) {
        container = document.createElement('div');
        container.id = 'toast-container';
        document.body.appendChild(container);
    }

    const toast = document.createElement('div');
    toast.className = 'toast';
    
    let borderLeftColor = 'var(--primary)';
    let iconColor = 'var(--primary)';
    let svgIcon = `<svg class="icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><line x1="12" y1="16" x2="12" y2="12"></line><line x1="12" y1="8" x2="12.01" y2="8"></line></svg>`;

    if (type === 'success') {
        borderLeftColor = 'var(--success)';
        iconColor = 'var(--success)';
        svgIcon = `<svg class="icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M22 11.08V12a10 10 0 1 1-5.93-9.14"></path><polyline points="22 4 12 14.01 9 11.01"></polyline></svg>`;
    } else if (type === 'danger' || type === 'error') {
        borderLeftColor = 'var(--danger)';
        iconColor = 'var(--danger)';
        svgIcon = `<svg class="icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><line x1="15" y1="9" x2="9" y2="15"></line><line x1="9" y1="9" x2="15" y2="15"></line></svg>`;
    } else if (type === 'warning') {
        borderLeftColor = 'var(--danger)';
        iconColor = '#b45309';
        svgIcon = `<svg class="icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24"><path d="M10.29 3.86L1.82 18a2 2 0 0 0 1.71 3h16.94a2 2 0 0 0 1.71-3L13.71 3.86a2 2 0 0 0-3.42 0z"></path><line x1="12" y1="9" x2="12" y2="13"></line><line x1="12" y1="17" x2="12.01" y2="17"></line></svg>`;
    }

    toast.style.borderLeftColor = borderLeftColor;

    let actionBtnHtml = '';
    if (actionText) {
        actionBtnHtml = `<button class="toast-btn">${actionText}</button>`;
    }

    toast.innerHTML = `
        <div class="d-flex align-center gap-2 flex-1">
            <div style="color: ${iconColor}; display: flex; align-items: center; flex-shrink: 0;">
                ${svgIcon}
            </div>
            <div class="toast-content">
                <span class="toast-title">${title}</span>
                <span class="toast-msg">${message}</span>
            </div>
        </div>
        <div class="d-flex align-center">
            ${actionBtnHtml}
            <button class="toast-close">×</button>
        </div>
    `;

    container.appendChild(toast);

    const closeToast = (el) => {
        el.style.animation = "confirmFadeOut 0.25s ease forwards";
        setTimeout(() => { if (el.parentElement) el.remove(); }, 250);
    };

    if (actionText && actionCallback) {
        const btn = toast.querySelector('.toast-btn');
        btn.addEventListener('click', () => {
            actionCallback();
            closeToast(toast);
        });
    }

    const closeBtn = toast.querySelector('.toast-close');
    closeBtn.addEventListener('click', () => closeToast(toast));

    setTimeout(() => {
        if (toast.parentElement) closeToast(toast);
    }, 10000);
}

// Load settings
async function loadPengaturan() {
    try {
        const res = await window.electronAPI.invokeBackend("get_pengaturan", {});
        if (res && (res.data || res.status === "success")) {
            pengaturan = res.data || res;
            renderPengaturanForm();
        }
    } catch (err) {
        showToast("Error loading pengaturan:", err, "danger");
    }
}

// render pengaturan
function renderPengaturanForm() {
    if (!pengaturan) return;
    const fields = {
        "nama_sekolah": pengaturan.nama_sekolah,
        "nama_kepala": pengaturan.nama_kepala_sekolah,
        "nip_kepala": pengaturan.nip_kepala_sekolah,
        "nama_bendahara": pengaturan.nama_bendahara,
        "nip_bendahara": pengaturan.nip_bendahara,
        "nama_pengurus_barang": pengaturan.nama_pengurus_barang,
        "nip_pengurus_barang": pengaturan.nip_pengurus_barang,
        "alamat_sekolah": pengaturan.alamat_sekolah
    };

    for (const [id, value] of Object.entries(fields)) {
        const el = document.getElementById(id);
        if (el) {
            el.value = value || "";
        }
    }
}

        pengaturan.nip_pengurus_barang = document.getElementById("nip_pengurus_barang").value;

}

window.updateFileList = function() {
    const input = document.getElementById('bkuFile');
    const display = document.getElementById('selected-files');
    if (!input || !display) return;

    display.innerHTML = ""; 

    if (input.files.length > 0) {
        if (input.files.length > 12) {
            showToast("Error!", "Maksimal 12 file!", "danger");
            try { input.value = ''; } catch(e) {}
            return;
        }

        const title = document.createElement('div');
        title.style.cssText = 'margin: 8px 0 6px 0; font-weight: bold; font-size: 13px; color: #333;';
        title.innerHTML = `📄 File Terpilih (${input.files.length}/12):`;
        display.appendChild(title);

        Array.from(input.files).forEach((file, index) => {
            const item = document.createElement('div');
            item.style.cssText = 'background: #f8f9fa; padding:8px 12px; margin-bottom:6px; border-radius:6px; display:flex; justify-content:space-between; align-items:center;';
            item.innerHTML = `<div style="overflow:hidden; white-space:nowrap; text-overflow:ellipsis; max-width:85%">${index+1}. ${file.name}</div><div style="color:#666; font-size:12px">${(file.size/1024).toFixed(0)} KB</div>`;
            display.appendChild(item);
        });
    }
};

// Extract Data BKU
async function uploadBKU() {
    const fileInput = document.getElementById("bkuFile");
    const files = fileInput.files;

    if (!files.length) {
        showToast("Error!", "Pilih file PDF terlebih dahulu!", "danger");
        return;
    }

    if (files.length > 12) {
        showToast("Error!", "Maksimal pengunggahan adalah 12 file sekaligus!", "danger");
        return;
    }

    showToast("info!", `Sedang memproses ${files.length} file BKU...`, "info");

    try {
        const fileListPayload = await Promise.all(
            Array.from(files).map(f => fileToBase64(f))
        );

        const res = await window.electronAPI.invokeBackend("extract_bku", { files: fileListPayload });

        if (res && res.status === "success") {
            bkuData = res.data;

            showToast(
                "Berhasil!",
                `Berhasil extract ${bkuData.transaksi ? bkuData.transaksi.length : 0} data BKU dari ${files.length} file.`,
                "success"
            );

            renderBKUResult(null, true);
        } else {
            showToast("Error!", res.detail || res.message || "Error extract BKU", "danger");
        }
    } catch (err) {
        showToast("Error!", "Error: " + err.message, "danger");
    }
}

// Save data BKU
async function saveTransaksi() {
    if (!bkuData || !bkuData.transaksi || bkuData.transaksi.length === 0) {
        showToast("Warning!", 'Tidak ada data BKU untuk disimpan', "warning");
        return;
    }

    showToast("info!", 'Meminta preview sebelum menyimpan...', "info");
    try {
        const preview = await window.electronAPI.invokeBackend("save_transaksi", { ...bkuData, force: false });

        if (preview && preview.status === 'preview') {
            const wouldDelete = preview.would_delete_rows || 0;
            const wouldDeleteBpu = preview.would_delete_bpu_rows || 0;
            const wouldInsert = preview.would_insert || 0;

            let msg = `Aksi ini akan menghapus ${wouldDelete} baris data Buku Kas Umum dan memasukkan ${wouldInsert} baris data Buku Kas Umum yang baru.`;
            if (wouldDeleteBpu > 0) msg += `<br><br><span style="color: red; font-weight: bold;">Peringatan:</span> Ini juga akan menghapus ${wouldDeleteBpu} data BPU yang mungkin sudah diperbaiki!`;
            msg += '<br><br>Apakah Anda yakin ingin melanjutkan?';

            const yakin = await showCustomConfirm("Konfirmasi Simpan", msg);
            if (!yakin) {
                showToast("info!", 'Simpan dibatalkan oleh pengguna.', "info");
                return;
            }

            const result = await window.electronAPI.invokeBackend("save_transaksi", { ...bkuData, force: true });

            if (result && result.status === "error") {
                showToast('Error!', result.detail || result.message || 'Error menyimpan transaksi', "danger");
                return;
            }

            showToast('Berhasil!', `Berhasil menyimpan ${result.saved_count || 0} data BKU`, "success");

            try {
                const trxRes = await window.electronAPI.invokeBackend("get_transaksi", {});
                const transactions = trxRes.data || [];
                
                bkuData = { 
                    transaksi: transactions, 
                    kwitansi_list: bkuData?.kwitansi_list || [] 
                };
                
                renderBKUResult();
            } catch (refreshErr) {
                showToast('Warning!', 'Data disimpan tapi gagal refresh: ' + refreshErr.message, "warning");
                renderBKUResult();
            }

        } else if (preview && preview.status === 'success') {
            showToast('Berhasil!', `Berhasil menyimpan ${preview.saved_count || 0} data BKU`, "success");
        } else {
            showToast('Error!', preview?.detail || preview?.message || 'Respon preview tidak dikenal', "danger");
        }

    } catch (err) {
        console.error('saveTransaksi error:', err);
        showToast('Error!', 'Error: ' + (err.message || err), "danger");
    }
}

    if (display) display.innerHTML = '';

}

function resetBkuSelection() {
    const input = document.getElementById('bkuFile');
    const display = document.getElementById('selected-files');
    if (input) try { input.value = ''; } catch(e) {}
    if (display) display.innerHTML = '';

async function extractBHP() {
    const input = document.getElementById('bhpFile');
    if (!input || !input.files.length) { showToast('Error!', 'Pilih file BHP/BHM terlebih dahulu!', "danger"); return; }
    if (input.files.length > 24) { showToast('Error!', 'Maksimal 24 file!', "danger"); return; }

    const jenis = document.getElementById('bhp-type') ? document.getElementById('bhp-type').value : 'BHP';
    showToast('Info!', `Sedang mengekstrak ${input.files.length} file ${jenis}...`, 'info');

    try {
        const fileListPayload = await Promise.all(
            Array.from(input.files).map(f => fileToBase64(f))
        );

        const res = await window.electronAPI.invokeBackend("extract_bhp", { files: fileListPayload, jenis });
        if (!res || res.status === "error") {
            showToast('Error!', res.detail || res.message || 'Gagal ekstrak BHP/BHM', "danger");
            return;
        }

        const bhpData = res.data || [];
        
        const applyRes = await window.electronAPI.invokeBackend("apply_bhp", { items: bhpData, preview: false });
        if (!applyRes || applyRes.status === "error") {
            showToast('Error!', applyRes.detail || applyRes.message || 'Gagal menerapkan perbaikan BHP/BHM', "danger");
            return;
        }

        const deleted = applyRes.deleted || 0;
        const inserted = applyRes.inserted || 0;
        window.lastBhpAppliedCount = inserted;

        try {
            const trxRes = await window.electronAPI.invokeBackend("get_transaksi", {});
            bkuData = { transaksi: trxRes.data || [] };
        } catch (err3) {
            showToast("Error!", 'Gagal refresh transaksi: ' + err3.message, "warning");
        }

        showToast('Berhasil!', `Ekstrak selesai — ${deleted} baris diganti, ${inserted} baris dimasukkan`, "success");
        const resDiv = document.getElementById('bhp-result');
        if (resDiv) resDiv.innerHTML = `<div class="alert alert-info">Ekstrak selesai — <strong>${deleted}</strong> baris diganti, <strong>${inserted}</strong> baris dimasukkan.</div>`;
        renderBKUResult();

    } catch (err) {
        showToast('Error!', 'Error: ' + (err.message || err), "danger");
    }
}

function normalizeNoBukti(v) {
    if (!v) return '';
    return v.toString().replace(/\s|\W/g, '').toLowerCase();
}

function normalizeDateToDDMMYYYY(input) {
    if (!input) return '';
    if (/^\d{2}[\-\/]\d{2}[\-\/]\d{4}$/.test(input)) {
        return input.replace(/\//g, '-');
    }
    if (/^\d{4}\-\d{2}\-\d{2}$/.test(input)) {
        const parts = input.split('-');
        return `${parts[2]}-${parts[1]}-${parts[0]}`;
    }
    const tmp = new Date(input);
    if (!isNaN(tmp.getTime())) {
        const dd = String(tmp.getDate()).padStart(2, '0');
        const mm = String(tmp.getMonth() + 1).padStart(2, '0');
        const yyyy = tmp.getFullYear();
        return `${dd}-${mm}-${yyyy}`;
    }
    return '';
}

function applyBhpToBKU(bhpItems) {
    if (!bkuData || !Array.isArray(bhpItems) || bhpItems.length === 0) return 0;
    let modified = 0;
    bhpItems.forEach(b => {
        const nbRaw = (b.no_bukti || b['No Bukti'] || b['no_bukti'] || '').toString().trim();
        if (!nbRaw) return;
        const nb = normalizeNoBukti(nbRaw);
        const matches = bkuData.transaksi.filter(t => normalizeNoBukti(t.no_bukti) === nb);
        const candidates = matches.length ? matches : bkuData.transaksi.filter(t => normalizeNoBukti((t.no_bukti||'')).includes(nb) || nb.includes(normalizeNoBukti((t.no_bukti||''))));

        candidates.forEach(t => {
            const newTanggal = normalizeDateToDDMMYYYY(b.tanggal || b.Tanggal || b.tanggal_bhp || b.tgl || '');
            if (newTanggal) t.tanggal = newTanggal;

            const rawHarga = b.harga_satuan || b['Harga Satuan'] || b.harga || b.harga_satuan_bhp || '';
            const h = parseFloat(String(rawHarga).toString().replace(/[^0-9\-\.]/g, ''));
            if (!isNaN(h) && h > 0) {
                t.harga_satuan = h;
            }

            const vol = parseFloat(t.volume) || 0;
            const harga = parseFloat(t.harga_satuan) || 0;
            t.pengeluaran = vol * harga;
            modified++;
        });
    });
    return modified;
}

// Download Kwitansi
async function downloadKwitansi(id, noBukti = "") {
    showToast("Info", "Mengunduh SPJ...", "info");

    try {
        const res = await window.electronAPI.invokeBackend("get_kwitansi_pdf", { id });

        if (!res || res.status === "error" || (!res.pdf_base64 && !res.data)) {
            showToast("Error!", res?.detail || res?.message || "Gagal mengunduh SPJ", "danger");
            return;
        }

        const safeFileName = noBukti ? noBukti.replace(/\//g, "-") : id;
        downloadBase64File(res.pdf_base64 || res.data, `SPJ-${safeFileName}.pdf`, "application/pdf");
        showToast("Berhasil!", "SPJ berhasil diunduh", "success");

    } catch (err) {
        showToast("Error!", "Error: " + err.message, "danger");
    }
}

// Import referensi kegiatan from Excel
async function importMasterKegiatan() {
    const fileInput = document.getElementById("excelFile");
    if (!fileInput.files.length) {
        showToast("Error!", "Pilih file Excel terlebih dahulu!", "danger");
        return;
    }

    showToast("Info", "Sedang import...", "info");
    try {
        const data = await fileInput.files[0].arrayBuffer();
        const workbook = XLSX.read(data, { type: "array" });
        const sheet = workbook.Sheets[workbook.SheetNames[0]];
        const json = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet);

        if (!json.length) {
            showToast("Error!", "File Excel kosong atau format salah", "danger");
            return;
        }

        const masterData = json
            .map(row => ({
                kode_kegiatan: String(row.kode_kegiatan || row["Kode Kegiatan"] || row["kode"] || "").trim(),
                nama_kegiatan: String(row.nama_kegiatan || row["Nama Kegiatan"] || row["nama"] || "").trim()
            }))
            .filter(item => item.kode_kegiatan && item.nama_kegiatan);

        const res = await window.electronAPI.invokeBackend("save_master_kegiatan_bulk", { data: masterData });

        if (res && res.status === "success") {
            const total = res.saved_count ?? res.total ?? 0;
            showToast("Berhasil!", `Berhasil import ${total} data referensi kegiatan`, "success");
            loadMasterKegiatan();
        } else {
            showToast("Error!", "Gagal import data referensi kegiatan", "danger");
        }

    } catch (err) {
        showToast("Error!", "Error: " + err, "danger");
    }

    fileInput.value = "";
}

// Import referensi rekening belanja from Excel
async function importMasterRekeningBelanja() {
    const fileInput = document.getElementById("excelFileRekening");
    if (!fileInput || !fileInput.files.length) {
        showToast("Error!", "Pilih file Excel terlebih dahulu!", "danger");
        return;
    }

    showToast("Info", "Sedang import rekening belanja...", "info");
    try {
        const data = await fileInput.files[0].arrayBuffer();
        const workbook = XLSX.read(data, { type: "array" });
        const sheet = workbook.Sheets[workbook.SheetNames[0]];
        const json = XLSX.utils.sheet_to_json(sheet);

        if (!json.length) {
            showToast("Error!", "File Excel kosong atau format salah", "danger");
            return;
        }

        const masterData = json
            .map(row => ({
                kode_rekening_belanja: String(row.kode_rekening_belanja || row["kode_rekening_belanja"] || row["kode"] || "").trim(),
                nama_rekening_belanja: String(row.nama_rekening_belanja || row["nama_rekening_belanja"] || row["nama"] || "").trim(),
                rekap_rekening_belanja: String(row.rekap_rekening_belanja || row["rekap_rekening_belanja"] || "").trim(),
                nilai_kapitalisasi_belanja: parseFloat(row.nilai_kapitalisasi_belanja || row["nilai_kapitalisasi_belanja"] || 0) || 0
            }))
            .filter(item => item.kode_rekening_belanja && item.nama_rekening_belanja);

        const res = await window.electronAPI.invokeBackend("save_master_rekening_belanja_bulk", { data: masterData });

        if (res && res.status === "success") {
            const total = res.saved_count ?? res.total ?? 0;
            showToast("Berhasil!", `Berhasil import ${total} data rekening belanja`, "success");
            loadMasterRekeningBelanja();
        } else {
            showToast("Error!", "Gagal import data rekening belanja", "danger");
        }

    } catch (err) {
        showToast("Error!", "Error: " + err, "danger");
    }

    fileInput.value = "";
}

// Load referensi rekening belanja
async function loadMasterRekeningBelanja() {
    try {
        const res = await window.electronAPI.invokeBackend("get_master_rekening_belanja", {});
        if (res && res.status === "success") {
            masterRekeningBelanja = res.data || [];
            renderMasterRekeningBelanja();
        }
    } catch (err) {
        showToast("Error!", "Error loading master rekening belanja: " + err, "danger");
    }
}

// render referensi rekening belanja
function renderMasterRekeningBelanja(data = null) {
    const container = document.getElementById("master-rekening-list");
    const countSpan = document.getElementById("master-rekening-count");
    if (!container) return;
    const list = data || masterRekeningBelanja;
    countSpan.innerText = list.length;

    if (list.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = "<p style='text-align:center; padding:20px;'>Belum ada data referensi rekening belanja.</p>";
        return;
    }

    let html = `
        <div class="table-scroll">
            <table>
                <thead>
                    <tr>
                        <th style="width:180px">Kode Rekening</th>
                        <th>Nama Rekening</th>
                        <th style="width:180px">Rekap</th>
                        <th style="width:140px; text-align:right">Nilai Kapitalisasi</th>
                        <th style="width:120px; text-align:center">Aksi</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
    `;

    list.forEach(item => {
        html += `
            <tr>
                <td><strong>${item.kode_rekening_belanja}</strong></td>
                <td>${item.nama_rekening_belanja}</td>
                <td>${item.rekap_rekening_belanja || "-"}</td>
                <td style="text-align:right">${item.nilai_kapitalisasi_belanja ? Number(item.nilai_kapitalisasi_belanja).toLocaleString('id-ID') : '-'}</td>
                <td style="text-align:center">
                    <button class="btn btn-danger btn-sm" onclick="deleteMasterRekening(${item.id})">Hapus</button>
                </td>
            </tr>
        `;
    });

    html += "</tbody></table></div>";
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

async function deleteMasterRekening(id) {
    const yakin = await showCustomConfirm(
        "Konfirmasi Hapus",
        "Hapus rekening belanja ini?"
    );
    if (!yakin) return;

    try {
        const response = await window.electronAPI.invokeBackend("delete_master_rekening", { id });

        if (!response || response.status === "error") {
            throw new Error(response?.detail || response?.message || "Gagal menghapus rekening.");
        }

        masterRekeningBelanja = masterRekeningBelanja.filter(item => item.id !== id);
        renderMasterRekeningBelanja(); 
        showToast("Berhasil!", "Data Referensi Rekening Belanja berhasil dihapus.", "success");

    } catch (error) {
        showToast("Gagal!", `Gagal menghapus: ${error.message}`, "danger");
    }
}

async function generateRekapTransaksi(forceDetails = false) {
    showToast('info!', 'Memproses rekap...', 'info');
    try {
        const include = forceDetails || document.getElementById('rekap-include-details')?.checked;
        const thp = document.getElementById('rekap-thp-select') ? document.getElementById('rekap-thp-select').value : 'all';
        const res = await window.electronAPI.invokeBackend("get_rekap_transaksi", { details: include ? 1 : 0, thp });
        
        if (!res || res.status !== 'success') throw new Error(res?.detail || res?.message || 'Gagal memproses rekap');
        renderRekapTransaksi(res.data, include);
        showToast('Berhasil!', 'Rekap selesai', 'success');
    } catch (err) {
        showToast('Error!', err.message || err, 'danger');
    }
}

function getPlaceholderCode(text) {
    if (!text) return "";
    return text.trim().toUpperCase().replace(/[^A-Z0-9]/g, '_');
}

function renderRekapTransaksi(data, withDetails = false) {
    const container = document.getElementById('rekap-result');
    if (!container) return;

    if (!Array.isArray(data) || data.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = "<p style='text-align:center; padding:20px;'>Tidak ada data rekap.</p>";
        return;
    }

    let html = `
        <div class="table-container">
            <table>
                <thead>
                    <tr>
                        <th>Uraian Rekap</th>
                        <th style="width:250px; background:#eef;">Kode Template (Copy Ini)</th>
                        <th style="text-align:right">Jumlah</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
    `;

    data.forEach(group => {
        const code = getPlaceholderCode(group.rekap);

        html += `
            <tr>
                <td><strong>${group.rekap}</strong></td>
                
                <td style="background:#f4faff;">
                    <code 
                        style="cursor:pointer; display:block; padding:4px; background:#fff; border:1px solid #ddd; font-size:11px; color:#d63384;"
                        onclick="navigator.clipboard.writeText('${code}'); showToast('Info','Kode ${code} disalin!', 'info');"
                        title="Klik untuk copy"
                    >${code}</code>
                </td>

                <td style="text-align:right">Rp ${Number(group.total_pengeluaran || 0).toLocaleString('id-ID')}</td>
            </tr>
        `;

        if (withDetails && Array.isArray(group.items) && group.items.length > 0) {
            html += `
                <tr>
                    <td colspan="3"> <div style="padding:10px; background:#fafafa; border-radius:8px; margin-top:6px;">
                            <strong>Detail:</strong>
                            <table style="width:100%; margin-top:8px; border-collapse:collapse;">
                                <thead>
                                    <tr>
                                        <th>No Bukti</th>
                                        <th>Kode Rekening</th>
                                        <th>Harga Satuan</th>
                                        <th style="text-align:right">Jumlah</th>
                                    </tr>
                                </thead>
                                <tbody>
            `;
            group.items.forEach(it => {
                html += `
                    <tr>
                        <td>${it.no_bukti}</td>
                        <td>${it.kode_rekening}</td>
                        <td>Rp ${Number(it.harga_satuan || 0).toLocaleString('id-ID')}</td>
                        <td style="text-align:right">Rp ${Number(it.pengeluaran || 0).toLocaleString('id-ID')}</td>
                    </tr>
                `;
            });
            html += `</tbody></table></div></td></tr>`;
        }
    });

    html += "</tbody></table></div>";
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

async function processTemplateExcel() {
    const fileInput = document.getElementById('template-file');
    const thpSelect = document.getElementById('rekap-thp-select');

    if (!fileInput.files || fileInput.files.length === 0) {
        showToast('Warning', 'Silakan pilih file template Excel (.xlsx) dulu.', 'warning');
        return;
    }

    const originalFile = fileInput.files[0];
    showToast('Info', 'Sedang memproses Excel...', 'info');

    try {
        const filePayload = await fileToBase64(originalFile);
        const res = await window.electronAPI.invokeBackend("generate_rekap_excel", {
            file: filePayload,
            thp: thpSelect.value
        });

        if (!res || res.status === 'error' || (!res.file_base64 && !res.data)) {
            throw new Error(res?.detail || res?.message || 'Gagal memproses file');
        }

        const originalName = originalFile.name; 
        let cleanName = originalName.replace(/[_-\s]*template[_-\s]*/gi, '');

        if (cleanName.length < 5) {
            cleanName = `Rekap_Hasil_${thpSelect.value}.xlsx`;
        }

        downloadBase64File(
            res.file_base64 || res.data,
            cleanName,
            "application/vnd.openxmlformats-officedocument.spreadsheetml.sheet"
        );
        
        showToast('Success', `File berhasil diunduh: ${cleanName}`, 'success');
    } catch (err) {
        console.error(err);
        showToast('Error', err.message, 'danger');
    }
}

async function deleteMasterKegiatan(kode) {
    const yakin = await showCustomConfirm(
        "Konfirmasi Hapus", 
        `Apakah Anda yakin ingin menghapus master kegiatan <strong>${kode}</strong>?`
    );
    if (!yakin) return;
    
    try {
        const res = await window.electronAPI.invokeBackend("delete_master_kegiatan", { kode_kegiatan: kode });
        if (res && res.status === "success") {
            showToast("Berhasil!", "Data Referensi Kegiatan berhasil dihapus.", "success");
            loadMasterKegiatan();
        }
    } catch (err) {
        showToast("Gagal!", "Error: " + err, "danger");
    }
}

// Tab switching
window.switchTab = function(tab) {
    activeTab = tab;

    document.querySelectorAll(".tab-btn").forEach(btn => {
        btn.classList.toggle("active", btn.dataset.tab === tab);
    });
    
    document.querySelectorAll(".tab-content").forEach(content => {
        content.classList.toggle("active", content.id === `tab-${tab}`);
    });

    const searchInput = document.getElementById("globalSearchInput");
    
    if (searchInput) {
        searchInput.value = ""; 
        const searchContainer = searchInput.parentElement; 

        switch (tab) {
            case 'master':
                searchInput.placeholder = "Cari data...";
                if(searchContainer) searchContainer.style.display = 'flex';
                if (typeof renderMasterKegiatan === 'function') renderMasterKegiatan(masterKegiatan);
                break;

            case 'rekening':
                searchInput.placeholder = "Cari data...";
                if(searchContainer) searchContainer.style.display = 'flex';
                if (typeof renderMasterRekeningBelanja === 'function') renderMasterRekeningBelanja(masterRekeningBelanja);
                break;

            case 'data-bku':
                searchInput.placeholder = "Cari data...";
                if(searchContainer) searchContainer.style.display = 'flex';
                if (typeof renderBKUResult === 'function') {
                    renderBKUResult(bkuData ? bkuData.transaksi : []);
                }
                break;

            case 'kwitansi':
                searchInput.placeholder = "Cari data...";
                if(searchContainer) searchContainer.style.display = 'flex';
                if (typeof renderKwitansiList === 'function' && bkuData) {
                    renderKwitansiList(bkuData.kwitansi_list);
                }
                break;

            case 'pengaturan':
            case 'pemda':
            case 'rekap':
            case 'bku':
            case 'update':
                if(searchContainer) searchContainer.style.display = 'none';
                break;

            default:
                searchInput.placeholder = "Cari data...";
                if(searchContainer) searchContainer.style.display = 'flex';
                break;
        }
    }

    if (tab === 'rekap') {
        const includeDetails = document.getElementById('rekap-include-details')?.checked || false;
        if (typeof generateRekapTransaksi === 'function') {
            generateRekapTransaksi(includeDetails);
        }
    }
};

function renderMasterKegiatan(data = null) {
    const container = document.getElementById("master-list");
    const countSpan = document.getElementById("master-count");
    if (!container) return;
    const list = data || masterKegiatan;
    countSpan.innerText = list.length;

    if (list.length === 0) {
        container.innerHTML = "<p style='text-align:center; padding:20px;'>Belum ada data referensi kegiatan.</p>";
        return;
    }

    let html = `
        <div class="table-scroll">
            <table>
                <thead>
                    <tr>
                        <th style="width:150px">Kode Kegiatan</th>
                        <th>Nama Kegiatan</th>
                        <th style="width:100px; text-align:center">Aksi</th>
                    </tr>
                </thead>
                <tbody>
    `;

    list.forEach(item => {
        html += `
            <tr>
                <td><strong>${item.kode_kegiatan}</strong></td>
                <td>${item.nama_kegiatan}</td>
                <td style="text-align:center">
                    <button class="btn btn-sm btn-danger" onclick="deleteMasterKegiatan('${item.kode_kegiatan}')">
                    Hapus
                    </button>
                </td>
            </tr>
        `;
    });

    html += "</tbody></table></div>";
    container.innerHTML = html;
}

function renderBKUResult(filteredData = null, isExtractMode = false) {
    const tbody = document.getElementById("bku-body");
    const btnArea = document.getElementById("bku-action-buttons");

    if (!tbody) return;

    const dataToRender = filteredData || (bkuData ? bkuData.transaksi : []);

    if (dataToRender.length === 0) {
        tbody.innerHTML = `
            <tr>
                <td colspan="9" style="text-align:center; padding:30px; color: var(--text-muted);">
                    <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="width: 40px; height: 40px; stroke: #cbd5e1; fill: none; stroke-width: 1.5; stroke-linecap: round; stroke-linejoin: round; margin-bottom: 10px;">
                        <circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle>
                        <line x1="12" y1="8" x2="12" y2="12"></line>
                        <line x1="12" y1="16" x2="12.01" y2="16"></line>
                    </svg>
                    <br>Tidak ada data ditemukan.
                </td>
            </tr>
        `;
        if (btnArea) btnArea.style.display = "none";
        return;
    }

    if (isExtractMode && dataToRender.length > 0) {
        if (btnArea) btnArea.style.display = "block";
    } else {
        if (btnArea) btnArea.style.display = "none";
    }

    tbody.innerHTML = '';
    dataToRender.forEach(item => {
        const row = `
            <tr>
                <td>${item.no_bukti || '-'}</td>
                <td>${item.tanggal || '-'}</td>
                <td>${item.kode_kegiatan || '-'}</td>
                <td>${item.uraian || '-'}</td>
                <td style="text-align:center;">${item.volume || 0}</td>
                <td style="text-align:center;">${item.satuan || ''}</td>
                <td style="text-align:right; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums;">${formatCurrencyLabel(item.harga_satuan || 0)}</td>
                <td style="text-align:right; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums; font-weight: 500;">${formatCurrencyLabel(item.pengeluaran || 0)}</td>
                <td style="text-align:center; vertical-align: middle;">
                    <button class="btn btn-outline-danger btn-sm" 
                            onclick="deleteTransaksi('${item.no_bukti}')" 
                            title="Hapus Transaksi"
                            style="display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 6px; padding: 6px 10px;">
                        <svg viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="width: 14px; height: 14px; stroke: currentColor; fill: none; stroke-width: 2; stroke-linecap: round; stroke-linejoin: round;">
                            <polyline points="3 6 5 6 21 6"></polyline>
                            <path d="M19 6v14a2 2 0 0 1-2 2H7a2 2 0 0 1-2-2V6m3 0V4a2 2 0 0 1 2-2h4a2 2 0 0 1 2 2v2"></path>
                        </svg>
                        Hapus
                    </button>
                </td>
            </tr>
        `;
        tbody.insertAdjacentHTML('beforeend', row);
    });
} 

function parseTanggalToDate(value) {
    if (!value) return null;
    const parts = value.split("-");
    if (parts.length !== 3) return null;

    const [dd, mm, yyyy] = parts.map(Number);
    const d = new Date(yyyy, mm - 1, dd);

    return isNaN(d.getTime()) ? null : d;
}

function formatTanggalID(value) {
    const d = parseTanggalToDate(value);
    return d ? d.toLocaleDateString("id-ID") : "-";
}

async function downloadBAST(noBukti) {
    if (!noBukti) {
        showToast("Error!", "No bukti belum tersedia", "danger");
        return;
    }

    try {
        const res = await window.electronAPI.invokeBackend("generate_bast_pdf", { no_bukti: noBukti });
        if (!res || res.status === "error" || (!res.pdf_base64 && !res.data)) {
            showToast("Error!", res?.detail || res?.message || "Gagal mengunduh BAST", "danger");
            return;
        }

        downloadBase64File(res.pdf_base64 || res.data, `BAST-${noBukti.replace(/\//g, "-")}.pdf`, "application/pdf");
        showToast("Berhasil!", "BAST berhasil diunduh", "success");
    } catch (err) {
        showToast("Error!", "Error: " + err.message, "danger");
    }
}

// --- Fungsi Render Kwitansi ---
function renderKwitansiList(list) {
    const container = document.getElementById("kwitansi-list");

    if (!Array.isArray(list)) {
        container.innerHTML = `
            <div class="alert-danger" style="display:flex; align-items:center; gap:8px; padding: 15px; border-radius: 8px;">
                <svg class="icon" viewBox="0 0 24 24" style="width: 20px; height: 20px; stroke: currentColor; fill: none; stroke-width: 2;"><circle cx="12" cy="12" r="10"></circle><line x1="15" y1="9" x2="9" y2="15"></line><line x1="9" y1="9" x2="15" y2="15"></line></svg>
                Data kwitansi tidak valid
            </div>`;
        return;
    }

    list.sort((a, b) => {
        const dA = parseTanggalToDate(a.tanggal);
        const dB = parseTanggalToDate(b.tanggal);
        if (dA && dB && dA.getTime() !== dB.getTime()) return dA - dB;
        return (a.nomor_kwitansi || "").localeCompare(b.nomor_kwitansi || "", undefined, { numeric: true });
    });

    let html = `
        <style>
